                                                  Sandwich
Ingredients                         Directions
6 slices bacon                      Preheat an oven to 325 degrees F (165 degrees C).
1/2 cup mayonnaise
1/4 cup sour cream                  Place the bacon in a large, deep skillet; cook over medium-high
2 tablespoons hot buffalo wing      heat, turning occasionally, until evenly browned. Drain the bacon
sauce                               slices on a paper towel-lined plate.
1 loaf Italian bread, cut in half
                                    Combine the mayonnaise, sour cream, and hot sauce in a small
lengthwise
                                    bowl. Spread both halves of the bread with the mayonnaise
3 cups shredded meat from a
                                    mixture. Place chicken on bottom half of bread; layer bacon and
rotisserie chicken
                                    cheese on top of chicken. Fold the top half of the bread over the
8 slices pepperjack cheese          bottom, and wrap with foil.
1/4 cup roasted red bell peppers,
drained and sliced (optional)       Bake for 20 to 25 minutes. Add a layer of red pepper slices on top
                                    of the bacon and cheese, if desired. Cut into slices, and serve.

Ingredients                           Directions
1 slice focaccia bread, cut in half   In a small bowl, mix together mayonnaise and basil pesto; spread
horizontally                          onto one half of bread. Spread other half with sun-dried tomato
1 tablespoon mayonnaise               pesto. Arrange roasted red peppers on bottom piece. Cover with

2 tablespoons sun-dried tomato
pesto

                                                        Harvey Ham Sandwiches
Ingredients                            Directions
1 (6 pound) bone-in ham               Place the ham in a large pot or slow cooker, and fill with enough
1 (8 ounce) jar yellow mustard        water to cover. Bring to a boil, then reduce the heat to low, and
1 pound brown sugar                   simmer for 8 to 10 hours. Remove the meat from the water, and
24 dinner rolls, split                allow to cool. If it has cooked long enough, it will fall into pieces as
                                      you pick it up.

                                      Pull the ham apart into shreds once it is cool enough to handle. It
                                      doesn't have to be tiny shreds. Place the shredded ham into a slow
                                      cooker. Stir in the mustard and brown sugar, cover, and set to Low.
                                      Cook just until heated. Serve on dinner rolls. We don't use any other
                                      sandwich toppings with it, but that is a personal choice.

1 tablespoon minced jalapeno    with black pepper, garlic powder, and salt.
pepper
1/2 tablespoon honey mustard    Spread egg mixture on four of the bread slices. Layer with baby
1/2 teaspoon black pepper       spinach leaves and tomato slices, and top with remaining slices of
1/2 teaspoon garlic powder      bread.
1 pinch salt (optional)
8 slices bread
24 leaves baby spinach
8 slices tomato

(such as SPAMВ®), grated
